### ERA 1

sericulture = {
	# Unlocks Mulberry Groves PM on Rice Farms
	era = era_1
	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/sericulture.dds"
	category = production
	can_research = no

	modifier = {
		building_silk_plantation_throughput_add = 0.25
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 1

		# SITAI BEGIN information:sericulture.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_b3099e12aacdd05f [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_b3099e12aacdd05f_productive_option_need_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# technology_sericulture_throughput [market]; 锁定真实throughput机会成员与本国实际使用匹配；同州、同建筑、低级PM与目标产出不跨成员拼接，未知局部退出
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes AND = { market = { mg:silk = { market_goods_delta < 0 } } any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_silk_plantation level >= 1 } } } } add = 0.2 }
			}
			min = 1
			max = 1.2
		} }
		# SITAI END information:sericulture.ai_weight
	}
}

enclosure = {
	# Unlocks construction of Farms and Plantations
	era = era_1
	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/enclosure.dds"
	category = production

	ai_weight = {
		value = 3 # Very important tech in general

		# SITAI BEGIN information:enclosure.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_b88ed7c281b8c91f [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_b88ed7c281b8c91f_productive_option_need_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# technology_enclosure_supply_wine_pressure [market]; wine缺口与高价升档同一供给原因；不同替代方式或同配方多种产出不累加
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:wine mg:wine = { market_goods_delta < 0 market_goods_pricier > 0.25 } } } } add = 0.2 }
				# technology_enclosure_supply_tobacco_pressure [market]; tobacco缺口与高价升档同一供给原因；不同替代方式或同配方多种产出不累加
				else_if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:tobacco mg:tobacco = { market_goods_delta < 0 market_goods_pricier > 0.25 } } } } add = 0.2 }
				# technology_enclosure_supply_tea_pressure [market]; tea缺口与高价升档同一供给原因；不同替代方式或同配方多种产出不累加
				else_if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:tea mg:tea = { market_goods_delta < 0 market_goods_pricier > 0.25 } } } } add = 0.2 }
				# technology_enclosure_supply_sugar_pressure [market]; sugar缺口与高价升档同一供给原因；不同替代方式或同配方多种产出不累加

manufacturies = {
	# Unlocks Mercantilism Law
	# Unlocks Food Industry, Textile Mills, Furniture Manufacturies, Glassworks, Tooling Workshops, Paper Mills
	era = era_1
	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/manufacturies.dds"
	category = production

	ai_weight = {
		value = 3 # Very important tech in general

		# SITAI BEGIN information:manufacturies.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_b577f5b9d1fca758 [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_b577f5b9d1fca758_productive_option_need_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# technology_manufacturies_supply_tools_pressure [market]; tools缺口与高价升档同一供给原因；不同替代方式或同配方多种产出不累加

shaft_mining = {

	unlocking_technologies = {
		enclosure
		manufacturies
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 2 # Important tech in general

		# SITAI BEGIN information:shaft_mining.ai_weight

cotton_gin = {
	era = era_1
	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/cotton_gin.dds"
	category = production

	modifier = {
		building_cotton_plantation_throughput_add = 0.25
	}

	unlocking_technologies = {
		manufacturies
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 1

		# SITAI BEGIN information:cotton_gin.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_c9999a5620730e5e [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_c9999a5620730e5e_productive_option_need_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# technology_cotton_gin_throughput [market]; 锁定真实throughput机会成员与本国实际使用匹配；同州、同建筑、低级PM与目标产出不跨成员拼接，未知局部退出
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes AND = { market = { mg:fabric = { market_goods_delta < 0 } } any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_cotton_plantation level >= 1 } } } } add = 0.2 }
			}
			min = 1
			max = 1.2
		} }
		# SITAI END information:cotton_gin.ai_weight
	}
}

lathe = {
	# Unlocks Lathes PM in Furniture Manufacturies
	era = era_1
	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/lathe.dds"
	category = production

	unlocking_technologies = {
		cotton_gin
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 1

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					 has_strategy = ai_strategy_industrial_expansion
					 has_strategy = ai_strategy_resource_expansion
				}
			}
			add = 1
		}

		# SITAI BEGIN information:lathe.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_8364f99fc32101e3 [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_8364f99fc32101e3_productive_option_need_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# technology_lathe_productive_ready [market]; 锁定真实productive_ready机会成员与本国实际使用匹配；同州、同建筑、低级PM与目标产出不跨成员拼接，未知局部退出
